## Document 003: 10月FOMC会议点评：暂停加息、保持谨慎，2026或仍有1次加息

2026年10月29日

《经济边际回升，政策效果待释放—宏
观经济点评》-2026.10.21 
《财政支出仍有发力空间 —宏观经济
点评》-2026.8.22 
《经济放缓，新动能逆势提速—宏观经
济点评》-2026.8.18

暂停加息、保持谨慎，2026或仍有1次加息 
——10月FOMC会议点评

潘纬桢（分析师）     
panweizhen@kysec.cn 
证书编号：S0790524040006

事件：美联储于北京时间10月29日凌晨2点发布10月FOMC会议声明，宣布
保持利率水平不变。 
⚫ 声明及发布会要点 
1. 美联储在此次会议上宣布维持利率水平不变，维持在 3.75%—4.0%区间。资
产负债表政策方面，将会继续坚持充裕准备金制度。 
2. 维持利率水平不变，出现2张鹰派反对票。在声明中，美联储的措辞同9月
份相比有些许变化，主要体现在对美国经济的评估更加积极。美联储认为经济活
动正以强劲的速度扩张，尽管地缘政治发展等因素导致不确定性依然较高，但消
费保持韧性，生产率增长和资本投资强劲。相较于9月，美联储对美国经济的评
估有所提升；就业方面，就业增长与劳动力增长保持同步，失业率变化不大；通
胀方面，通胀依旧高企。此外，声明还表示更多信息的积累将有助于委员会确定
适当的政策立场，意味着政策调整的节奏可能放缓。此外，本次声明再次出现2
张反对票要求加息，显示出美联储内部的鹰派声音仍然较强。 
3. 发布会上，沃什整体表态略偏鸽派，表示当前货币政策正在发挥作用，将使
经济更具韧性，并依然实现价格稳定。在发言稿以及问答中，沃什表示价格稳定
的目标就是以PCE衡量的2%，没有软性通胀目标，过去两天FOMC的讨论主
要集中在价格稳定一侧。美联储的工作是确保某些部门的相对价格变化不会在整
个经济中扩大，并确保通胀预期保持锚定。在生产率提升的推动下，美国经济增
长较快。此外，FOMC的议程将会改革，从2027年开始，会有1场FOMC会
议专门讨论广泛的货币政策战略，这场会议将不会做出利率决议，也不会发布政
策声明。到2028年，将会有2场会议专门讨论战略，6场会议用于制定政策。 
⚫ 美联储将保持谨慎，2026或再加息一次 
第一， 9月加息后，美联储面临的压力略有缓和，连续加息的紧迫性下降。且
10月 FOMC临近中期选举，此时加息可能会将美联储置于政治纠纷之中。11
月3日美国即将举行中期选举，10月FOMC会议上的任何加息/降息，可能都会
引发总统的不满甚至攻击，从而将美联储拖入不必要的政治漩涡中。对2002年
及以后的中期选举前的FOMC会议进行复盘，除去2022年由于通胀高企进行了
75bp加息之外，美联储在中期选举前1个月基本都保持利率水平不变。 
第二，中期选举后地缘政治不确定性可能增加，美联储或将保持谨慎。从现有
信息来看，特朗普打算在中期选举后对伊朗发动大规模打击，目标或涉及伊朗的
基础设施，届时若战争出现超预期变化，则全球油价或将再度上升，而美国通胀
的上升压力也将快速增加。在这种背景下，美联储或对后续政策保持谨慎，以避
免出现通胀的超预期抬升及民众通胀预期的失控。 
基准情形下，我们认为美联储或在2026年12月再加息一次，2027年加息情况
则高度取决于美伊局势的变化，以及油价对美国通胀的传导效应。 
⚫ 短期内市场风险偏好保持平稳，关注科技公司财报 
决议及发布会后，市场风险偏好相对平稳。美股方面，在声明及发布会后，美股
先跌后涨，最终道琼斯指数收涨0.19%，纳斯达克指数收涨0.27%；美债方面，
10年期美债收益率总体上涨，目前维持在5.0%上方；美元方面，发布会后有所
回落，目前稳定在102以上；黄金则出现上涨，目前稳定在4080美元/盎司左右。
我们认为尽管沃什表态相对偏鸽，但会议节奏基本符合市场预期，对风险偏好
影响相对较小。往后看，还需要高度关注美国科技公司的财报表现，以及美伊
局势的变化可能导致的油价波动。CME期货显示12月加息概率为78%左右。 
⚫ 风险提示：国际局势紧张引发通胀超预期，美国经济超预期衰退。

事件：美联储于北京时间10月29日凌晨2点发布10月FOMC会议声明，宣
布保持利率水平不变。 
1、 声明及发布会要点 
1.1、 维持利率水平不变，再次出现2张鹰派反对票 
美联储在此次会议上宣布维持利率水平不变，维持在3.75% - 4.0%区间。资产负
债表政策方面，将会坚持充裕准备金制度。 
在声明中，美联储的措辞同9月份相比有些许变化，主要体现在对美国经济的
评估更加积极。此外还出现2张反对票要求加息，显示美联储内部鹰派观点仍较强。
经济方面，美联储认为经济活动正以强劲的速度扩张，尽管地缘政治发展等因素导
致不确定性依然较高，但消费保持韧性，生产率增长和资本投资强劲。相较于9月，
美联储对美国经济的评估有所提升；就业方面，就业增长与劳动力增长保持同步，
失业率变化不大；通胀方面，通胀依旧高企。此外，声明还表示更多信息的积累将
有助于委员会确定适当的政策立场，意味着会议召开的节奏可能放缓。此外，本次
声明再次出现2张反对票， 分别是库克 （Lisa D. Cook）和哈马克（Beth M. Hammack），
这2位票委要求加息25bp，显示出美联储内部的鹰派声音仍然较强，后续再度加息
的可能性相对较高。 
表1：美联储10月决议内容相较于9月略有变化 
声明要点 10月会议表述 9月会议表述 
投票结果 
联邦公开市场委员会以 10比 2的投票结果批准发布以下
声明。 
联邦公开市场委员会以 12比 0的投票结果批准发布以下声
明。 
货币政策行
动 
为支持美联储的双重使命，委员会决定将联邦基金利率目
标区间维持在 3.75%至 4%不变。委员会将继续奉行维持
银行体系充足储备的政策。 
为支持美联储的双重使命，委员会决定将联邦基金利率目
标区间上调 0.25个百分点至 3.75%至 4%。委员会将继续
维持银行体系充足的储备金。 
美国经济 
经济活动正以强劲的速度扩张。尽管地缘政治发展等因素
导致不确定性依然较高，但国内消费依然保持韧性。生产
率增长强劲，资本投资也十分活跃。 
经济活动正稳步扩张。尽管地缘政治局势发展等因素导致
不确定性依然较高，但国内消费依然保持韧性。生产率增
长强劲，资本投资也十分活跃。 
就业与通胀 
就业增长与劳动力增长保持同步，失业率变化不大。通胀
水平依然高企。 
就业增长与劳动力增长保持同步，失业率变化不大。通胀
依然高企。 
货币政策目
标 
更多信息的积累将有助于委员会确定适当的政策立场， 
以确保通胀率及时回升至委员会设定的 2%目标。委员会
将致力于实现物价稳定。 
今天的政策行动将有助于更快地实现委员会设定的 2%的
目标。委员会将致力于稳定物价。 
资料来源：美联储、开源证券研究所 
1.2、 发布会上，沃什并未给出新增政策信息，整体基调略显鸽派  
从发布会上沃什的发言和表态来看，整体表态略偏鸽派，表示当前货币政策的
正在发挥作用，将使经济更具韧性，并依然实现价格稳定。 
（1）本次货币政策决议方面，美联储主席沃什表示美联储主要是通过质询现实
（interrogating reality） ，以衡量现实中到底发生了什么。尽管实体经济活动正在朝着
正确的方向前进，但通胀太高了（inflation is too high），过去两天FOMC的讨论主
要集中在价格稳定一侧。美联储的工作是确保某些部门的相对价格变化不会在整个
经济中扩大（do not broaden out across the economy ），并确保通胀预期保持锚定。9
月份加息以后，整体金融状况有所收紧，但不能称之为偏紧（ unable to call

### Page 4

宏观经济点评 
请务必参阅正文后面的信息披露和法律声明 4 / 8 
them tight）。价格稳定的目标就是以PCE衡量的2%，没有软性通胀目标。此外，利
率水平的抬升直接或间接地影响着几乎所有事情，AI投资或也受到一定影响。 
（2）关于劳动力市场与通胀水平，沃什认为当前失业率低于一年前，非农就业
增长明显高于2025年的速度。四周移动平均的首次申请失业救济人数继续处于较低
水平。因此，在美联储双重使命的就业方面，情况相当积极。不过通胀水平已经连
续6年高于美联储的目标。 
（3）关于美国经济，沃什表示经济增长有所加强（strengthened）。2026年上半
年的GDP增幅从此前估计的1.8%上修至2.4%， 而第三季度似乎带来了更快的增长，
这主要由健康的消费者支出（增长率可能高于3%）以及企业投资（可能以双位数速
度增长）所主导。资本支出占GDP的比重处于历史高位。高科技投资引领了加速，
但在其他领域也有所扩大，这些趋势值得关注。生产率在过去几年里一直在经济中
走高，因此经济增长更高了。 
（4）沃什还表示，美联储还将会对FOMC的日程安排做出改变。从2027年开
始，虽然还有8次FOMC会议，但会有1场会议专门讨论广泛的货币政策战略，这
场会议将不会做出利率决议，也不会发布政策声明。到2028年，将会有2场会议专
门讨论战略，6场会议用于制定政策。从而搜集更丰富的信息以评估经济进展，并重
新思考重大问题。此外，11月16日将举行关于生产率工作小组的听证会。 
2、 美联储将保持谨慎，2026或再加息一次 
在9月FOMC 会议上加息后，由于 9 月 CPI 数据反弹，市场一度认为美联储将
会开启连续加息。但随着沃什在IMF会议上表示9月加息以来的全球经济表现“大体
符合”美联储预期，市场开始定价 10月不加息。不过随着中期选举投票结束，地缘
政治不确定性增加，我们认为美联储或将在12月份再次加息。具体而言： 
第一，9月加息后，美联储面临的压力略有缓和，连续加息的紧迫性下降。且
10月FOMC临近中期选举，此时加息可能会将美联储置于政治纠纷之中。在9月
FOMC会议上，沃什表示“很难将广泛的金融状况描述为限制性的，9月加息是减少
了一些政策宽松”1，从9月FOMC的会议纪要来看2，美联储许多官员支持加息，
主要出于风险管理考虑，即所谓的“保险式”加息，应对需求超预期或供给端再次
受到冲击的风险。这也意味着，在9月加息之后，美联储再连续加息的紧迫性下降。
此外，还有一个需要注意的点，11月3日美国即将举行中期选举，对美联储来说，
10月 FOMC会议上的任何加息/降息，可能都会引发总统的不满甚至攻击，从而将
美联储拖入不必要的政治漩涡中。事实上，对2002年及以后的中期选举前的FOMC
会议进行复盘，除去2022年由于通胀高企进行了75bp加息之外，美联储在中期选
举前1个月基本都保持利率水平不变。而在特朗普执政的背景下，我们认为美联储
或更不愿意加息，以避免触怒特朗普。

1 https://wallstreetcn.com/articles/3781921?keyword=FOMC 
2 https://wallstreetcn.com/articles/3783183?keyword=%E7%BA%AA%E8%A6%81

3、 短期内市场风险偏好保持平稳，关注科技公司财报 
决议及发布会后，市场风险偏好相对平稳。美股方面，在声明及发布会后，美
股先跌后涨，最终道琼斯指数收涨0.19%，纳斯达克指数收涨0.27%；美债方面，10
年期美债收益率总体上涨，目前维持在5.0%上方；美元方面，发布会后有所回落，
目前稳定在102以上；黄金则出现上涨，目前稳定在4080美元/盎司左右。我们认为
尽管沃什表态相对偏鸽，但会议节奏基本符合市场预期，对风险偏好影响相对较小。
往后看，还需要高度关注美国科技公司的财报表现，以及美伊局势的变化可能导致
的油价波动。CME期货显示12月加息概率为78%左右。

4、风险提示 
（1）若国际局势紧张，可能导致大宗商品价格上涨，引发美国通胀超预期；（2）若
美国劳动力市场超预期下跌，可能引发美国经济衰退，需进一步降息。

## Document 058: 2026年7-9月期GDP（1次速報）予測 ～前期比年率＋1.5％を予想～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年7-9月期GDP（1次速報）予測 ～前期比年率＋1.5％を予想～.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-10-30

Extraction status: ok

日本

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9番 1号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー

2026年 10月 30日 全7頁

2026年 7-9月期 GDP（1次速報）予測 
～前期比年率＋1.5％を予想～ 
個人消費は小幅減だった一方、設備投資と輸出が実質GDPを押し上げ

経済調査部 エコノミスト 畑中 宏仁 
  チーフエコノミスト 神田 慶司

[要約] 
◼ 11月 16日公表予定の 2026年 7-9月期の GDP1次速報では、実質 GDPが前期比年率＋
1.5％（前期比＋0.4％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長を予想する。個人消費は小幅
に減少したが、設備投資と輸出の増加が実質GDPを押し上げたようだ。 
◼ 個人消費はサービスが増加したものの、財が減少した影響の方が大きかったと予想す
る。設備投資は 4-6 月期に特殊要因で減少していた反動もあって増加したとみられる。
公共投資は減少、政府消費は増加したと見込んでいる。 
◼ 輸出と輸入はサービスが減少した一方、財が増加したと予想する。代替調達の進展に
よる鉱物性燃料輸入増加は実質 GDPを押し下げたが、石油国家備蓄の大規模放出が行
われなかったことで公的在庫変動が実質 GDP成長率を押し上げた分が相殺したようだ。

### Page 2

2 / 7

1．2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比年率＋1.5％を予想 
11月 16日公表予定の 2026年 7-9月期の GDP1次速報では、実質 GDPが前期比年率＋1.5％
（前期比＋0.4％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長を予想する（10月30日正午までに公表された
基礎統計に基づく）。個人消費は小幅に減少したが、設備投資と輸出が実質GDPを押し上げたよ
うだ。 
個人消費はサービスが増加したものの、財が減少した影響の方が大きかったと予想する。設
備投資は4-6月期に特殊要因で減少していた反動もあって増加したとみられる。 
輸出と輸入はサービスが減少したものの、財が増加したと予想する。中東産原油の代替調達
の進展による鉱物性燃料輸入の増加は実質 GDPを押し下げたが、石油国家備蓄の大規模放出が
行われなかったことで公的在庫変動が実質GDP成長率を押し上げた分で相殺されたようだ。

図表 2：実質 GDP成長率と需要項目別寄与度の推移

民需：個人消費、住宅投資は減少の一方、設備投資は大幅な増加を予想 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％と予想する。4-6月期のインバウンド（個人消費の控除項目）の実
績が上方改定されるであろうことを踏まえると、2 四半期連続のマイナスとなると見込んでい
る。サービスは増加したものの、耐久財と半耐久財、非耐久財がそろって減少したとみている。
耐久財では、自動車税の環境性能割廃止やエアコンの省エネ基準の引き上げの影響もあって 4-
6 月期に増加していた自動車や家電への支出が減少したようだ。半耐久財・非耐久財では衣服
やたばこ、ガソリンなどが増加したものの、食料品などが減少したとみられる。サービスは外
食や鉄道旅客、娯楽などが増加したようだ。 
住宅投資は前期比▲1.8％と予想する。国土交通省によると、7月の建設工事費デフレーター
（住宅総合、大和総研による季節調整値）が前月比＋6.8％と高い伸びを示す中にあっても、7、
8 月平均の新設住宅着工戸数（国土交通省による季節調整値）は、分譲住宅の増加に支えられ
-1.0

### Page 3

1

3 / 7

4-6月平均比＋1.0％となった（巻末の関連指標②上段右）。しかし、進捗ベースで計上される
GDP統計上は、建設資材等の価格上昇の影響により実質では減少したとみられる。 
設備投資は前期比＋2.0％と予想する。建設投資は減少したものの、機械投資と知的財産生
産物投資（ソフトウェア、研究開発など）が増加したようだ。研究開発投資の増加には、4-6
月期に特殊要因で研究開発サービスの受取（設備投資の控除項目）が大幅に増加したことの反
動も含まれる。経済産業省によると、資本財総供給指数は、国産品と輸入品が共に増加し、7-
9 月期は前期比＋4.9％となった（巻末の関連指標②中段左）。一方、建設投資は、国土交通省
によると、民間非居住用建築・土木の出来高（名目額、大和総研による季節調整値）の7、8月
平均は 4-6月平均比＋1.2％となったが、7月の建設工事費デフレーター（非住宅総合、大和総
研による季節調整値）は前月比＋7.3％と大きく上昇しており、実質では減少したとみられる。 
民間在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.0％ptと予想する。仮置き値の原材料在庫は同＋0.1％pt、
仕掛品在庫は同▲0.1％pt となった。その他では製品在庫が同＋ 0.1％pt、流通在庫が同▲
0.1％ptだったとみられる。

公需：公共投資は減少、政府消費は増加と予想 
公共投資は前期比▲1.0％と予想する。国土交通省によると、7、8 月平均の公共工事出来高
（名目額、大和総研による季節調整値）は 4-6月平均比▲2.2％と減少傾向にある（巻末の関連
指標②中段右）。25年度補正予算に盛り込まれた公共事業の執行が一服したとみられる。加え
て、工事費の上昇も重しとなっている。 
政府消費は前期比＋0.2％と予想する。退職金支払いの増加や私立高校授業料の実質無償化、
公立小学校の給食費無償化もあって高い伸びとなった 4-6月期に続き、医療費の増加により 7-
9月期の政府消費も増加したとみられる。 
公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度＋0.3％ptと予想する。4-6月期と異なり、7-9月期には大規模
な石油の国家備蓄放出は行われず、公的在庫変動は実質GDP成長率を押し上げたとみられる。

外需：輸入増が輸出増を上回り、外需寄与度は▲0.2％ptを予想 
輸出は前期比＋0.5％と予想する。サービスは減少したものの、財は増加したと見込んでい
る。財では半導体等製造装置や化学製品、鉱物性燃料などが増加したようだ。サービスでは、
4-6月期に大幅に増加していた研究開発サービスの反動減に加え、産業財産権等使用料やイン
バウンドなども減少したとみている。 
輸入は前期比＋1.8％と予想する。輸出と同様、サービスは減少したものの、財は増加した
と見込んでいる。財では、代替調達の進展により鉱物性燃料が大きく増加したほか、電算機類
も増加したようだ。サービスではアウトバウンドは増加したものの、研究開発サービスや著作
権等使用料などが減少したとみられる。

### Page 4

1

4 / 7

輸入増が輸出増を上回り、純輸出（外需）の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は前期比▲0.2％
ptになったと予想する。

2．10-12月期の実質 GDPはおおむね横ばいを予想 
2026年 10-12月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比でおおむね横ばいになると見込んでいる。個人
消費は小幅に増加、設備投資は増加すると予想する。輸出はAI関連需要を背景とした米国向け
輸出を中心に増加するものの、輸入の伸びが上回り、外需はマイナスになるとみている。 
個人消費は小幅に増加すると予想する。厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計調査」の現金給与総額は
前年同月比で 3％を超える伸びが続いていることに加え、10月以降は各地で最低賃金が引き上
げられることから、名目賃金の高い伸びは続くだろう。一方、9月の消費者物価指数（除く生
鮮食品）は前年同月比＋2.1％と、8月から 0.4％pt伸び幅が拡大した。政策要因による影響を
差し引いたとしても、食料品等を中心に物価上昇率は高まっており、実質賃金上昇率を押し下
げるだろう。エネルギー価格についても、電気・ガス料金補助は12月使用分（1月請求分）か
ら再開されるものの、現行の補助は 9月使用分（10月請求分）で一旦終了するため、10-12月
期の物価は押し上げられる。加えて、ガソリン補助金の基準価格が12月以降引き上げられるこ
とや、燃料費・原料費調整制度によって資源価格高騰の影響がラグを持って電気・ガス料金に
反映されることも物価の押し上げ要因となろう1。 
設備投資は増加すると予想する。日本銀行「全国企業短期経済観測調査」 （日銀短観）によ
ると、9月調査時点での 2026年度の設備投資計画（全規模全産業、除く土地、含むソフトウェ
ア・研究開発）は前年度比＋9.3％と、前年の 9月調査時点（同＋9.5％）と同程度の伸びが示
された。建設資材価格と労務費の上昇が建設投資を下押しするものの、省力化投資やAI関連投
資といった設備投資の増加基調は続くと見込む。 
住宅投資は建設資材と労務費上昇の影響を受けて減少すると見込んでいる。公共投資は工事
費の高騰を背景に横ばい圏で推移すると予想する。政府消費は高齢化などに伴う医療費・介護
費増により増加すると見込んでいる。 
輸出は増加すると予想する。米国の設備投資は中東情勢をめぐる不透明感や金利上昇の中に
あっても、AI関連需要を背景に増加が続くだろう。米国内での設備投資の増加は米国向け輸出
の追い風となり、財輸出は増加すると見込んでいる。一方、インバウンドは中国政府による日
本への渡航自粛要請が続く中、小幅な増加にとどまるだろう。 
輸入は増加すると予想する。経済産業省によると、9月の原油調達について、バブ・エル・
マンデブ海峡経由で調達予定であった原油の輸送ルートをスエズ運河経由に切り替えたことで

1 12月以降の電気・ガス料金補助とガソリン補助金に関する政府の対応については、首相官邸「中東情勢を踏
まえた『緊急的激変緩和措置』の今後の対応についての会見」（2026年10月 29日）を参照。

### Page 5

1

5 / 7

到着が遅れた分は備蓄原油の既放出分で対応したという2。10月は前年平月比 10割の調達が実
現できる見通しとのことであり、11月以降も同量を確保できるのであれば、財輸入は押し上げ
られるだろう。 
リスク要因は中東情勢の悪化とAI関連需要の下振れである。中東情勢が悪化し、原油価格が
さらに高騰すれば、物価高を通じて個人消費を一段と下押しするだろう。また、足元の米国経
済では設備投資が景気を下支えしており、とりわけデータセンターや半導体などのIT関連投資
がけん引役となっている。10月半ばにかけて、一部AI関連企業の財務上の脆弱性が意識され、
株価が下落する局面があったが、こうした懸念がAI関連企業全体に広がれば、外部資金への依
存度が高い企業を中心に設備投資が鈍化し得る3。その場合、米国向け輸出が下押しされる可能
性があり、注意が必要だ。

2 詳細は、第 5回中東情勢に伴う重要物資の安定的な供給確保のためのタスクフォース（2026年 10月2日）
における経済産業省提出資料を参照。 
3 詳細は、矢作大祐・藤原翼「米国経済見通し CoreWeave問題をどうみるか」（大和総研レポート、2026年
10月22日）を参照。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.30-159.30

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1300-1.1450 
前週交易區間 
1.1358 1.1495

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.21-115.09.24 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.804% ~ 0.830%  
30天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95%  
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.84%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本周央行持續每日減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，帶動短
率靠向區間低位。 
週二央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.638%，略低於
市場預期，且較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.804%~0.830%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.69%，利率較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 及 5 年期指標券 A15108 均未成
交，週四等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.95%、1.84%。 
台股排擠效應、國際債市走勢偏空，加上油價續處高檔，通膨潛在威脅仍存，
12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持
高檔區間盤整 ， 預估5年券區間為 1.84%~1.90%，10年券區間為 1.95%~2.0%。

Upward earnings revisions; reiterate our Buy on both names — We raise EMC’s
2026/27 /28E earnings by 20%/31%/44% to factor in price hikes and enhanced
product mix. We thus raise EMC’s TP to NT$7,400 (28x 2027E EPS) from NT$6,000
(30x 2027E EPS). For TUC, we raise 2026/27 /28E earnings by 21%/29%/27% to
factor in price hike and enhanced product mix as well. We lift our TUC TP to
NT$2,300 (23x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,950 (25x 2027E EPS). We lower PE multiple

for both names given recent AI concerns amid the rate hike backdrop.

### Page 12

Taiwan PCB & Laminates
29 October 2026 Citi Research
12
Elite Material
Company description
Established in 1992 in Taoyuan, Taiwan, EMC is a global leading copper clad
laminate (CCL) and prepreg supplier for HDI and smartphone applications.
EMC has developed several green materials products, from Mid Loss, Low
Loss, Very Low Loss, Ultra Low Loss down to Extreme Low Loss categories. In
the past few years, EMC has continued to gain presence in AI datacenter,

networking and HPC sectors. In the AI server market, EMC is one of the key
suppliers to global AI GPU customers and a major supplier to US CSPs for AI
ASIC projects.
Investment strategy
We are positive over EMC’s long-term growth outlook as we believe its
technology leadership in high-end CCL should position it well to benefit from
the structural spec migration in PCB materials amid the AI demand boom. We
expect EMC to maintain its market share in AI GPU projects and to gradually

gain share in next-gen ASIC projects in 2026. Given its leading position in
high-end CCL, sufficient raw material support, expanding capacity and solid
earnings outlook, we rate EMC a Buy.
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$7,400, based on a target PE multiple of
28x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our target PE multiple, at the peak
of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings
outlook supported by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC

(high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec upgrade
for CCL, price hikes and product mix enhancement should help support its
margin expansion going forward. At our target price, the shares would trade at
55x/28x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which
we largely attribute to frequent debates over the spec upgrade for next-gen AI
servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope

for aggressive spec upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability
and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in the end.
However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec
upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth potential among AI servers of
different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-

expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg,
glass, copper foil, foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server
projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.
TUC

### Page 13

Taiwan PCB & Laminates
29 October 2026 Citi Research
13
Company description
TUC was established in 1974 and mainly produced optical glass initially. Since
1997, it started to provide copper clad laminate (CCL) and prepreg.
Headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, TUC has an extensive global service
network spanning mainland China, Japan, Korea, the US and Germany. It has
been specializing in high-speed CCL for networking and now has a strong
position in supplying 800G switch opportunities. The company also started

to supply AI ASIC from 2025.
Investment strategy
We expect increasing AI ASIC exposure for TUC as it continues to see new
project opportunities among US CSP customers, including the high-end CCL
market for Trainium chip. We like TUC as it has production expertise in high-
speed CCL products, especially in networking, such as 800G, and has long
built solid relationships with upstream high-end materials suppliers to ensure
raw material support. We rate TUC a Buy given meaningful 800G ramp from

2026E onwards and its increasing opportunities in AI projects within CSPs
and enterprise players.
Valuation
Our target price for TUC is set at NT$2,300, based on a target PE multiple of
23x on our 2027E EPS. We believe our target PE multiple, at the peak of TUC’s
PE average in the last upcycle, is justified by its strong margin expansion
potential on a higher sales mix from AI ASIC server/800G products and price
hikes on supply tightness. We believe TUC’s growth outlook is well supported

by AI ASIC and 800G with its capacity ramp in Thailand from 3Q26 onward.
At our target price, the shares would trade at 2026E/27E PB of 53x/23x.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates TUC High Risk given high share price volatility, which
we largely attribute to frequent debates over the spec upgrade for next-gen AI
servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope
for aggressive spec upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability

and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in the end.
However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec
upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth potential among AI servers of
different generations. As such, we do not assign a High Risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-
expected AI server/800G demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply

chain (e.g., glass, copper foil, foundry, or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in
key AI server projects; 5) flexible practice of OOC policy by end customers;
and 6) slow development of the PCB industry in Southeast Asia.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.
Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

not guaranteed. Private companies are generally not subject to reporting obligations, which may result in less publicly available
information. The information contained in this report may differ from information a private company may subsequently make
public or otherwise share with investors. Continuation of publishing private company reports or private company sections
within reports is at Citi's discretion, in accordance with internal policies.

## Document 156: 4541晟田 台新

Source PDF: all report pdf/4541晟田 台新.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為台新投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬台新投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113) 金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

晟田(4541)：航太及半導體客戶需求延續，帶動 2027 年獲利成長 
建議：中立轉買進

一、晟田為航太精密機械零件加工廠 
晟田成立於 1991 年 8 月，實收資本額 6.76 億元，截至
2Q26 底，員工約 686 位。主要從事航太零組件(包括發
動機機匣與懸塊、起落架)及機械零組件(包括半導體真
空泵浦零組件、食品飲料填充包裝設備零組件、工業自
動化精密傳動零組件等產品)之製造加工與銷售等業務。
2025 年前五大客戶為漢翔(2634)、日本 E 客戶、
SLS(Safran Landing Systems)、SPP (6355.JP)及長榮
航太(2645)。2026 年前 9 個月產品別營收佔比為航太零
組件(客供料) 48%，航太零組件(自購料) 25%，半導體
設備零組件 23%，其他 4%。

二 、 夏季電價及費用認列影響獲利 ， 預估3Q26 稅後 EPS 
1.26 元 
晟田 3Q26 營收 5.6 億元(+1.9%QoQ，+30.9%YoY)，
季增主因為受惠航太客供料客戶拉貨動能成長及半導體
客戶需求提升。獲利方面，儘管產品組合持續優化，惟
受到夏季電價及籌資相關費用認列影響，預估營業利益
1.01 億元(-6%QoQ，+97.1%YoY)。假設匯率持穩，預
估稅後淨利 0.85 億元(-1%QoQ，+34.4%YoY)， 稅後EPS 
1.26 元。 
三、產品組合持續優化，預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元 
展望 4Q26，半導體方面，考量客戶需求持續強勁，有
望帶動出貨動能；航太方面，波音(BA.US) 737 系列機
型月產能有望於 2026 年底自 42 架提升至 47 架，另，
根據空中巴士(EADSY.US)規劃，2026 年預計交付 870
架，截至 Sep-26 交付達成率約 6 成，預期 4Q26 將加
速交機 ， 帶動航太零組件拉貨需求 。 整體而言 ， 預估4Q26
營收 5.62 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+20.7%YoY)。獲利方面，
隨產品組合持續優化，且無電價因素干擾，預估營業利
益 1.05 億元(+3.9%QoQ，+28.6%YoY)。 假設匯率持穩，
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元。 
四、需求延續，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 4.51 元 
展望後市，儘管航太供應鏈仍受原物料短缺、人力及產
能瓶頸影響，惟波音及空巴在手訂單仍維持高位，顯示
終端需求具支撐。隨供應鏈瓶頸逐步緩解，預期將帶動
航太零組件拉貨需求回升，有利晟田營運成長。半導體
方面，受惠終端客戶持續擴大資本支出，帶動相關產品
需求穩定成長。整體而言，預估 2026 年營收 21.2 億元
(+29.1%YoY)。獲利方面，隨產品組合持續優化，預估
毛利率 25.1%，稅後淨利 3.1 億元(+122.9%YoY)，稅後
EPS 4.51 元。 
展望 2027 年，考量航空引擎零組件客戶需求強勁，晟
田規劃擴充相關產能，新廠有望於 2H27 投產；半導體
方面，持續優化相關產能，以因應客戶需求成長。整體
而言，預估 2027 年營收 23.5 億元(+10.7%YoY)。獲利
方面，考量新廠投產後折舊費用增加，預估毛利率年減
至 24.8%， 稅後淨利3.3 億元(+9%YoY)， 稅後EPS 4.58
元(以增資稀釋後股本計算)。 
五、中立轉買進理由 
評價方面，以 2027 年 EPS 估算，目前 PE 約為 13X，
介於晟田歷史區間 12X~20X 下緣 ， 調升至買進評等 ， 目
標價 75 元(2026F PE 17X)。

주가 추이

3Q 큰 폭의 판매 감소, 매출/영업이익 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영 업 이 익 은 각각 44.6조원(-4.5% 이하 Y oY)과 2.4조원
(-5.4%) 기록하며 시장 기대치 하회 전망. 3Q 매출액은 글로벌 판매 부진 지속
과 환율 하락, 그리고 친환경차 판매 성장 둔화 등의 영향에 기인. 특히, 3Q 판
매량은 918.7만대(-11.5% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동기 대비 큰 폭 감소하며 매출
액에 부정적 영향. 이는 7월과 8월 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생
산 물량 차질이 발 생 한 부분에 기인. 파업은 기존 물량/믹스 개선에 기 여 했 던 
글로벌 HEV 판매도 둔화(+3.4%)시키면서 매출에 부정적 영향을 심화. 
3Q 수 익 성 은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입에 따른 원가 상승 영향과 친환경차 
판매 감소에 따른 부정적 믹스 영향 불 가 피. 이에 시장 기대치인 3.0조원을 크
게 하회할 전망. 다만, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮아지면서 판매보증비 
외화환산손익 영향은 다소 개선(0.4%p) 예상되며, 관세 영향 또한 전년 동기 
대비 약 1조원(2.1%p) 감소하면서 수익성 악화 영향을 일부 만 회 할 전망.  
’27년 지연된 신차효과 가시화로 반등 여력 충분 
4Q부터는 3Q 파업 영향 등으로 지연된 신차 확대 본격화되고, 현지 수요 높은 
차종 중심의 물량 확대를 통해 매출 회복 및 이익 성장 가시화가 기대. 아 반 떼
와 투싼 FMC 확대로 국내 및 해외 판매 둔화가 점진적 만회되고, 지난 9월부
터 터키에서 아이오닉3 생산이 본격화되면서 유럽에서의 시장 부진 회복이 기
대. 또한, GV80과 G80 HEV 모델 출시로 내년부터 국내/북미 중심 판매 확대
되며 올해 부진했던 물량/믹스 개선에 기여할 것으로 기대. ’27년 HMGMA에
서 HEV 신차 생산 투입 예정되어 있어 상 대 적 으 로 높은 수요가 지속되는 북미
에서의 HEV 현지 생산 확대로 가동률 증 가 와 관세 부담 완화도 수익성 개선에 
기여할 것으로 기대. 
부정적 환율 고려 필요하나 ’27년 강한 증익 기대  
’27년 동사 영 업 이 익 추 정 치 는 12.4조원(+22.3%)으로 ’26년 감 익 에 서 ’27년 
증익 전환 예상. 최근 환율 추세 고려 시 내년 상 반 기 까 지 부정적인 환율 영향 
확대 가 능 성 존재하나, 기존 노후 차 종 에 서 현지 수요 대응 가능한 신차 중심 
생산/판매 확대로 물량/믹스 개선되며 부 정 적 환율 영향 상쇄 기대.  
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-11.8%)과 상반기 적 용 한 코 스 피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)에 따라 목 표 주 가 를 670,000원으로 하향. 그러나, 연 내 RMAC 운영 
성 과 와 RA 설립, 12 월 모셔널 자 율 주 행 상용화와 웨 이 모向 로 보 택 시 
양산/공급 등 미래 사 업 에 서 의 가 시 성 도 여전히 기대 가능한 부분.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계 
금융투자상품의 비중 96.4% 3.6% 0.0% 100.0%

## Document 201: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

(005930) 
삼성전자 
2026.09.29

반도체 손인준 
6154 / sij@eugenefn.com

긴 호흡으로 
 투자의견 STRONG BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 560,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 270,000원(9/28)

 삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익이 103.6조원(+16%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 메모리 가격 등 전반적인 반도체 업황에 대한 기존 가정은 대체로 
유지했으나, 최근 원화 강세에 따라 원/달러 환율 가정을 1,440원에서 
1,400원으로 낮추고 MX 사업부의 적자 확대를 반영해 실적 추정치를 하
향 조정했다. 또한 2Q26 재고가 3Q26 매출로 인식되는 과정에서 발생하
는 추가 성과급 충당금 부담도 반영했다.  
 DS 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 104.0조원(+17%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 서버 DRAM과 eSSD 중심의 가격 인상이 이어지며, DRAM과 
NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +17%, +18%QoQ 상승할 것으로 추정한
다. 1cnm 수율 개선과 HBM4의 빠른 램프업도 실적 개선에 기여할 전망이
다. Foundry/LSI는 HBM4 베이스다이 생산 확대, Groq 3 LPU 양산 및 선
단 공정 가격 인상에 힘입어 하반기 매출 성장이 예상된다. 다만 성과급 충
당금과 감가상각비 부담으로 영업적자 축소 폭은 제한적일 것으로 보인다. 
 DX 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 -1.8조원(적자지속qoq)을 기록할 것으로 추
정한다. 삼성전자는 메모리 가격 급등에도 제품 가격 인상을 제한하며 시장 
점유율 확대에 주력하고 있다. 이러한 가격 정책과 갤럭시 Z 폴드8 등 신제
품 흥행에 힘입어 판매량은 당초 예상치를 상회할 것으로 보인다. 다만 원
가 상승분의 전가가 제한되면서 영업적자 폭은 크게 확대될 전망이다.  
 삼성전자의 4Q26 영업이익은 114.9조원(+11%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. DRAM과 NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +11%, +12%QoQ 상승
하며 견조한 흐름을 이어갈 것으로 보인다. 환율 가정 조정에 따라 2026년
과 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 각각 365.2조원(+735%yoy), 595.8조원
(+63%YoY)으로 낮췄다. 4Q26~1Q27 범용 DRAM·NAND 및 2027년 
HBM 가격 협상 환경은 공급업체에 유리하게 전개되고 있어, 향후 가격 가
정의 추가 상향 가능성이 높다고 판단한다.  
 극심한 DRAM 공급 부족으로 인한 HBM 적층 단수 하향 조정(base die 수
요 증가), Memory Wall에 대응하기 위한 3D Stacked DRAM·zHBM 도입 
논의 등은 파운드리의 기술적 가치를 부각시키는 요인이다. 파운드리의 수
익성 회복 속도는 완만할 전망이지만, 메모리와 파운드리를 연계한 턴키 비
즈니스의 시너지는 AI 반도체 시장 내 경쟁력을 높이고, 메모리 시장 점유
율 확대에도 기여할 것으로 보인다. 적극적인 메모리 가격 인상 정책으로 
업계 최고 수준의 Blended ASP도 장기간 유지할 것으로 예상한다. 
 이제 메모리 업종 주가는 업황의 지속 가능성을 확인하고 꾸준한 주주환원
을 반영하며, 중장기적으로 점진적인 상승세를 이어갈 것으로 판단한다. 삼
성전자는 메모리·파운드리를 아우르는 턴키 역량과 가격 정책에 기반한 실
적 차별화를 통해 피어 그룹 내 경쟁력을 높여갈 것으로 보인다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

### Page 1

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  硬體‧美國

Flex 
(FLEX.O/ FLEX US)

預期資料中心電源需求為獲利加速成長之主要動能 
重要訊息 
Flex FY2Q27 財報與 FY3Q27 財測均優於市場共識 ，公司上修 FY2027 資料
中心年增率自 70%至 80%，收盤股價上漲約 12%。 
評論及分析  
FY2Q27 資料中心電源需求升溫， EPS 年增 49%至 1.18 美元並優於共識。
Flex FY2Q27 (2026 年 7–9 月)營收年增 25%、季增 7%至 84.9 億美元，主
要來自資料中心電源 需求延續 ；受惠於電源產品營利率 年增 5.5ppt s 至約
16.0%、帶動產品組合轉佳， Flex FY2Q27  EPS 年增 49%、季增 18%至
1.18 美元，大致符合凱基原預估、優於市場共識 12%。 
FY3Q27 資料中心成長性可望優於 FY2Q27，預估 EPS 年增 52%至 1.32 美
元。Flex 已於 10 月完成對 EPC Power 的收購，該公司可望貢獻單季營收約
2 億美元，並有助於 800V 架構及固態變壓器( SST)的技術能力。FY 3Q27 隨
Hyperscalers 與 Neo clouds 等客戶需求延續，凱基預估 Flex 營收年增
33%、季增 11%至 93.9 億美元，其中資料中心業務之營收年增率預期達
115%，其餘產品線則約 10%上下；受惠營利率較佳的資料中心業務貢獻提
升，FY3Q27  EPS 預估年增 52%、季增 11%至 1.32 美元。 
凱基預估 Flex FY2027/FY2028  EPS 為 5.24/8.80 美元。基於電源產品接單
成長性優於預期以及併購 EPC Power 也將挹注營收，Flex 上修 FY2027 資料
中心營收年增率 自 70%至 80%，FY2028 則維持年增 80%之展望，隱含
FY2027/FY2028 資料中心營收分別上修 約 6% 、營收占比預期達
33%/45%。基於 Flex 上修展望，凱基 將 Flex FY2027/ FY2028 EPS 自原預估
之 4.96/7.96 美元上修至 5.24/8.80 美元，分別年增 59%/68%。 
投資建議  
凱基維持「增加持股」評等，目標價上修至 176 美元 (20x FY2028F EPS) 。 
投資風險  
關稅戰惡化導致客戶下單意願降低； CPI 業務營運成長態勢不如預期。

5.24/8.80 美元。

### Page 2

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Flex 資料中心營收占比提升至 30%。Flex 三大產品線為 ITS (包含網通與智慧
生活，FY 2Q27 營收占比 39%；營利率 5.5%)、RMS ( 包含工業、汽車與醫
療，FY1Q27 營收占比 31%；營利率 6.4%)、CPI ( 資料中心業務，FY1Q27
營收占比 30%；營利率 10.7%)。各項業務之應用主要包含:  (1) 工業產品涵
蓋資本設備、工業裝置、再生能源、電源與電網邊緣等解決方案； (2) 車用
產品主要提供下一代移動方案、自駕、連網化 、電動化與智慧科技 ；(3) 健
康解決方案包含醫療裝置、醫療設備與藥物輸送系統； (4) 智慧生活包含消
費性電子、家電、消費性包裝、掃地機器人、微型移動裝置及音訊產品； (5) 
網通領域涵蓋通訊基礎設施與企業網路傳輸； (6) 資料中心業務 包含電源、
散熱、系統整合等產品與服務。   
Flex 目前的發展策略是結合 EMS 與產品的服務策略以提升接單能力，公司
表示自身是全球唯一具有提供從資料中心晶片到電網能力的供應商，除了可
提供嵌入式電源 (embedded power) 產品、管理伺服器 GPU 的電源，亦可
滿足客戶對資料中心內所有設施的電力與散熱需求，提供完整的客製化服
務，帶動公司資料中心營收占比自前一季之 28%提升至 FY2Q27 之 30%。

Flex FY2027/ FY2028 資料中心營收與獲利成長性可望優於產業平均 。Flex
計劃將 CPI 部門分拆成一家新的上市公司 Axiom，維持在 CY1Q27 完成分拆
的進度不變， Flex 自身將專注於醫療保健、機器人、自動化倉儲和網通等產
業，兩家公司可更聚焦 於彼此的業務並提升資金運用效率。公司 提及自身在
資料中心電源布局相對完整，且產品價格亦相對具有競爭優勢下，使公司在
電源產品接單成長性優於產業平均； 隨包含 Google 在內的 Hyperscalers 、
Colocations 及 Neo-clouds 等客戶對嵌入式電源與 系統級解決方案的需求升
溫，公司在此次會議將 FY2027 資料中心營收年增率自 70%上修至 80%、
FY2028 則維持年增 80%之展望，皆較 FY2026 資料中心業務營收年增 38%
至 66 億美元加速成長 ，並隱含 FY2027/FY2028 資料中心營收分別上 修約
6%、營收占比預期達 33%/45%。

### Page 3

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  3

獲利性部分， 受惠於電源產品營利率較佳、 FY2Q27 年增 5.5ppts 至約
16.0%，優於資料中心業務的平均營利率約 11%以及公司整體平均營利率
約 7%，公司上修 FY2027 資料中心業務之營利率約 1ppt 至 11.6%。凱基
預估隨資料中心營收貢獻提升， FY2027/FY2028 營 利占比分別達
49%/65%，為公司主要獲利動能。  
至於資料中心以外之 其餘應用，預期營收年增率 維持約 10%上下之水準。
目前歐洲工業需求略 有回溫，營收年增率可望維持約 10%上下；汽車產品
線則因目前全球汽車銷售量已逐步回升至過往 9,000 萬輛的正常水準，
FY2027 /FY2028 產品線成長性預期落在 5%以內；醫療設備則因客戶先前在
Covid -19 時期堆積的庫存水位過高，目前庫存去化雖逐步落底，但 需求端仍
相對平緩；網通領域則預期隨庫存去化力道放緩， FY2027 產品線營收將逐
步回溫，預期年增率 約 10%；消費性電子則因記憶體漲價、品牌廠將成本
轉嫁給消費者的影響， FY202 7 營收預期年減約 5-10%。 
Flex 評等維持「增加持股」，目標價上修至 176 美元。基於 Flex 上修資料
中心之營收與營 利率展望，凱基將 Flex FY2027/FY2028 EPS 自原預估之
4.96/7.96 美元上修至 5.24/8.80 美元，分別年增 59%/68%；相較市場共識
FY202 7/FY2028  EPS 預估為 4.48/6.82 美元，股價交易於約 12/14 倍之
FY2028 凱基與市場共識 EPS 預估。 
Flex 過往五年本益比多落於 5-20 倍之間，考量 (1) 公司在資料中心領域掌握
從晶片、液冷散熱、BBU 到電網的供應能力 ，布局相對多數同業更全面 ；(2) 
公司持續提升在 CSP 廠供應鏈之接單量，有助 FY2027 /FY2028 營收與獲利
加速成長； (3) 基於資料中心產品線營利率可望逐步提升至 10-15%、顯著
優於公司過往產品之 5%水準，且在獲利占比逐步提升至 50%以上的帶動
下，有助公司評價向散熱與電源同業靠攏。綜合上述 原因，評估 Flex 本益比
可望回升至區間 上緣之 20 倍上下浮動 ；基於 Flex 當前評價偏低 ，故維持
「增加持股」之投資評等，目標價隨 EPS 預估上修而調整至 176 美元 (20x 
FY202 8F EPS)。

### Page 7

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 7

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用
事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 - 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減
或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數 
股東治理分數 衡量公司用以反收購工具的有效性 
公司治理分數 衡量公司對最佳治理原則的承諾和有效性 
產品責任分數 衡量公司生產優質產品和服務的能力，且產品是否將客戶的健康、安全、整合性和數據隱私進行綜合考量 
社區關係分數 衡量公司對成為優良公民、保護公眾健康和尊重商業道德的承諾 
勞動力分數 衡量公司在員工工作滿意度、健康、工作場所的安全、多樣性、平等以及員工發展機會方面的成效 
資源使用指標 衡量公司在原物料、能源或水的使用效率，以及是否通過改進供應鏈來尋求更俱生態效率的解決方案

### Page 4

Monthly Economics
Sector Analysis
Personal Consumption Expenditures (PCE)
• We've lifted our Q3 expectation for real PCE on incoming data and a slightly firmer consumer
backdrop in the wake of recent revisions.
Consumer spending looks firmer in the third quarter, and we now expect real PCE to rise at a 3.4%
annualized clip. This upward revision reflects recent data, with August consumption coming in firm
with real spending rising by the fastest monthly clip in nearly two years, but also reflects a slightly

firmer consumer backdrop in the wake of the BEA's annual revisions released late September. Although
revisions did not materially alter spending, they lifted the level of household income in recent years,
which also boosted the personal saving rate. The trajectory of real income growth remains weak, but
the consumer still looks to be in a slightly stronger financial position than previously believed, which
helps explain recent resilience in spending and suggests consumers may have a bit more runway ahead.

Investment: Equipment, Intellectual Property Products and Inventories
• The AI build out will lead to another surge in business fixed investment spending in Q3, though the
effect on headline growth will be partly offset by stronger imports.
Business fixed investment spending continues to be supported primarily by the high-tech buildout.
We forecast real equipment investment to rise at around a 16% annualized clip in Q3, but part of
this boost to headline GDP growth will be offset by stronger import growth that is facilitating the

build out. Net imports of computers are tracking to surge in the third quarter, which will provide a
sizable lift to information processing equipment investment, or around half of overall equipment.
And while non-defense capital goods shipments (including aircraft) slipped in August, the near-term
growth trajectory remains supportive of a pickup in equipment. The rebuild in inventories we have
been anticipating is also tracking to be a bit stronger as businesses replenish drawn down product.

Investment: Residential
• The abrupt rise in mortgage rates is likely to dampen residential investment over the next few
quarters. Residential spending should find support next year as longer-term interest rates
moderate somewhat.
We are unlikely to see another positive residential investment reading in Q3. Rising mortgage rates

have sent a chill over builder outlooks, pushing sales expectations to their weakest level in more than
three years. While builders have responded with deeper price cuts, affordability pressures continue
to weigh on single-family sales and construction outlays. Apartment construction is simultaneously
soft amid rising financing costs and modest rent growth, and home improvement spending remains
constrained by high materials costs and interest rates. While residential investment is likely to remain

4 | Economics

### Page 5

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
a drag this year, we expect a slight moderation in longer-term interest rates in 2027, which would
provide a more supportive backdrop.
Investment: Nonresidential Structures
• We have not made material changes to our nonresidential structures forecast. Despite elevated
interest rates, data center and related spending should continue to support structures investment
through our forecast horizon.
Nonresidential structures spending has accelerated sharply, overwhelmingly driven by data centers

and electric power facilities. As data center construction expands geographically and demand rises
for semiconductors, high-tech capex has also started to generate positive spillovers for warehousing
and manufacturing construction. This buildout, which appears relatively insensitive to interest rates,
should be enough to sustain solid growth in structures investment even as spending across most
other categories remains essentially flat. Domestic energy is another green shoot, with elevated prices

supporting ongoing growth in active rotary rigs.

Net Exports & Trade
• An expected drag from trade in Q3 got larger in light of a near-record trade deficit in August, net
exports is now expected to lop off three full percentage points from third quarter growth.

Exports increased in August for the first time in four months, but the increase was more than offset by
an even larger increase in imports. The upshot was a widening in the trade deficit to a near-record gap
of more than $105 billion. Going into the report, we had a 1.9 percentage point drag from net exports
penciled in for Q3 GDP; we revised that to a full 3.0 percentage points drag in light of the record gap.
Even a modest narrowing in the final months of the year would likely mean a lift to headline GDP in

Q4, but the real story here is that trade will remain a headwind throughout the forecast period owing
largely to surging imports of tech-related products amid the AI boom.
Labor Market
• The jobs market remains stable. We continue to expect the unemployment rate to end the year at
4.2% and hold near that level in 2027.
We modestly revised down our near-term payroll growth forecast following the September jobs
report, but otherwise we have left our labor market outlook unchanged. Measures of labor demand

growth continue to point to a stable labor market. Job openings have inched higher on a year-ago
basis but remain broadly steady while initial jobless claims, WARN notices and Challenger job cut
announcements continue to signal low layoffs. Labor force participation appears to be improving after
its sharp pullback in the first half of the year. Looking ahead, we expect payroll growth to average
around 60K per month in 2027, roughly in line with our estimate of breakeven job growth. That should

Economics | 5

### Page 6

Monthly Economics
keep the unemployment rate near 4.2% in 2027 and leave labor market conditions broadly consistent
with "full employment." Notably, our expectation for labor cost growth of ~3.4% is the reason we
continue to reiterate that labor cost growth is not a contributor to the excess inflation seen in the
data.
Inflation
• We expect the core PCE deflator to rise 3.2% and 2.6% Q4/Q4 in 2026 and 2027, respectively.
Headline inflation remains heavily influenced by developments in the Middle East, with higher oil

prices lifting gasoline and diesel prices. Food inflation has been tame this year but looks poised to
strengthen as higher transportation and commodity prices work their way through supply chains.
Beyond food and energy, price pressures remain concentrated in a handful of categories, most notably
airfares and AI-related tech goods. Core goods inflation is showing signs of stabilizing, as waning tariff-
related price pressures have been offset by resilient demand tied to the AI buildout. We expect some

improvement here in 2027. Still-low shelter inflation and cooler price growth for airfares and health
care should help bring about some moderation in core services inflation next year. We expect the core
PCE deflator to rise 3.2% on a Q4-over-Q4 basis in 2026 before slowing to 2.6% in 2027.

Fiscal Policy
• Fiscal year (FY) 2026 ended on September 30, and the federal government tallied a $2.0 trillion
budget deficit (6.5% of GDP). We project the deficit to be unchanged in dollar terms in FY 2027

and a bit smaller as a share of GDP (~6%).
• Another continuing resolution to extend government funding past the December 11 deadline
seems like the most likely outcome at this time for the FY 2027 budget. It is possible Republicans
use the lame duck session of Congress to increase defense spending and raise the debt ceiling.
Looking ahead to the lame duck session of Congress that will follow the midterm elections, Congress
needs to pass a bill that extends government funding past December 11 to avert a government

shutdown. Another continuing resolution that punts the issue into Q1 seems like the most likely
outcome. Perhaps more interesting will be whether Republicans attempt to use budget reconciliation
to pass one more partisan piece of legislation. If they do, we suspect the two things at the top the
agenda will be more funding for national defense and immigration enforcement priorities as well as a
debt ceiling increase. For the latter, raising it in the lame duck would remove some leverage Democrats

could have in 2027 if they recapture one or both chambers. It is still very early, but we estimate the
debt ceiling "X date" by which Congress must act or face default is sometime in late Q3/early Q4 of
next year.
6 | Economics

### Page 7

Economic Outlook: October 2026 Economics
Monetary Policy & Interest Rates
• Our base case forecast is for the FOMC to hike the fed funds rate by 25 bps in December and
then keep the policy rate unchanged in 2027 at 4.00%-4.25%, with risks skewed toward additional
tightening.
As we go to print, the FOMC seems more likely than not to keep the fed funds rate unchanged in
October. But given the rationale under which the Fed started this tightening cycle and the forcefulness

with which Warsh has driven home his displeasure with elevated inflation, it seems entirely plausible
to us that October will be a live meeting. At a minimum, the recent inflation data seem to make a hike
in December a lock. Past December, the outlook will be highly dependent on future inflation data. Our
expectation is that core inflation will cool gradually in 2027, and we still do not see signs of demand-
side inflation in the data. But hiking cycles have an inertial component to them, and the recent inflation

data reset the clock to zero on the Fed seeing several good inflation prints in a row. This creates upside
risk to our base case for the fed funds rate.

International Developments
• Global growth remains resilient, leaving our 2026 and 2027 forecasts unchanged. Our inflation
forecasts are also unchanged, although risks of delayed price pass-through could prolong inflation
pressures into 2027.

• Monetary policy continues to lean toward tightening across advanced economies, while emerging
market central banks are increasingly cautious, either holding rates or easing more gradually as
inflation pressures rebuild.
The balance of risks to global inflation remains tilted to the upside, complicating the monetary policy
outlook even as growth has proven more resilient than expected. Elevated energy prices, particularly
for refined petroleum products, persistent geopolitical uncertainty and El Niño-related food price

risks could prolong inflationary pressures into 2027. While second-round effects still remain relatively
contained, repeated supply shocks are testing central banks’ willingness to look through the temporary
price increases, particularly where inflation expectations remain elevated, even as higher government
bond yields are already contributing to tighter financial conditions.
Across advanced economies, central banks face increasingly difficult trade-offs as persistent inflation

pressures limit the scope for policy accommodation. The Eurozone remains particularly vulnerable
to energy supply disruptions, with elevated natural gas prices and low storage levels adding to
inflation risks. We continue to expect a 25 bps European Central Bank hike in December, followed by a
prolonged hold through 2027. Fiscal concerns are also adding to pressure, particularly in France, where
the selloff in government debt has pushed yields higher. In the U.K., resilient activity and renewed

Economics | 7

### Page 8

Monthly Economics
inflation pressures similarly underpin our expectation for a Bank of England hike in November,
followed by a hold through 2027.
Elsewhere, policy paths are becoming more differentiated. After four hikes this year brought the Cash
Rate to 4.60%, we expect the Reserve Bank of Australia to remain on hold, although renewed inflation
pressures or stronger-than-expected activity could prompt further tightening. We also still expect
the Bank of Canada to hold at 2.25%, with subdued growth weighing against energy-driven inflation

risks, although incoming inflation data over the coming weeks will be critical in determining whether
persistent price pressures warrant tightening monetary policy perhaps as early as the December
meeting. Japan remains an exception to the broader hold outlook: while the yen's recovery provides
some relief from imported inflation, elevated energy prices and import dependence continue to pose
upside risks. We expect the Bank of Japan to hike again by year-end and now expect an additional 25

bps hike in Q3-2027, bringing the terminal rate to 1.75%.
The emerging market story has increasingly shifted from easing toward patience or selective
tightening. In Asia, China's weak domestic demand and persistent property-sector strains continue to
warrant policy support, with AI-related exports providing some offset. We expect a more gradual pace
of Reserve Requirement Ratio cuts alongside targeted monetary and fiscal measures. India faces the

opposite challenge, with elevated food and energy prices and rupee depreciation strengthening the
case for further Reserve Bank of India tightening. In Latin America, peso depreciation and elevated
energy costs pose upside inflation risks for Mexico, although limited exchange-rate pass-through
should allow Banxico to remain on hold while policymakers monitor underlying inflation and inflation
expectations. In Brazil, election-related uncertainty and renewed inflation pressures are likely to delay

further easing, with cuts expected to resume in Q1-2027 once policymakers see more convincing
evidence of disinflation.

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The six-month annualised growth rate in the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Leading Index, which indicates the
likely pace of economic activity relative to trend three to
nine months into the future, held at –0.03% in September,
unchanged from August.

The Leading Index continues to show moderately improved
momentum compared to the middle of the year, with the
latest reads pointing to growth holding at or slightly below
trend heading into late 2026/early 2027. Growth appears
to be stabilising at a slow pace rather than stalling. That
said, both the latest fuel prices and the RBA’s September
interest rate increase are still working their way through to
the economy with impacts likely to take some of the gloss off

growth near term. Overall, Westpac expects annual growth
to come in at 1.5% for calendar 2026, a below-trend result.
The Leading Index growth rate has firmed slightly over the
last six months, rising from –0.16% back in March to –0.03%
in September. The component detail shows mixed moves over
the period. Four components have contributed more positively
with reduced drags from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Unemployment Expectations Index (adding +0.23ppts to

the Index growth rate); the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Expectations Index (adding another +0.15ppts);
and the S&P/ASX200 (+0.15ppts) combining with a modest
lift in growth in US industrial production (adding +0.12ppts).
These positives have been partially offset by modest drags
from four previously stable components: dwelling approvals
(–0.34ppts); monthly hours worked (–0.08ppts); commodity
prices, measured in AUD terms (–0.07ppts) and the yield

spread (–0.05ppts).
Overall, the component mix speaks to a lack of direction.
Indeed, despite some large shocks over the last year, the
Leading Index growth rate has been relatively contained since
early 2025, holding in a narrow –0.3% to +0.5% range. This
compares to history which, outside of the COVID and GFC
shocks, has seen growth rates move between a much wider
–1.9% to +1.6% range. Of course, the apparent stability,
which is evident across all components of the index, may not

last but for now it suggests the economy has been stuck in a
bit of a holding pattern over the last two years.
Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend
The Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index of
Economic Activity is designed to assist in identifying
turning points in the economy. The index combines
variables that reflect different aspects of the economy
into a single index that generally produces a more reliable
cyclical indicator than any single component taken

individually. The Leading Index of Economic Activity
provides advance information on the state of the economy
and gives early warnings of cyclical turning points.
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting
**Please note that this will be the last Westpac press release for
the monthly Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index. We will
continue to publish regular bulletin updates through our westpaciq.
com.au website.

Editorial 
Policy, politics and earnings  
Strategy summary:  We retain our near -term defensive and 
diversified stance across assets, sectors and regions amid 
near-term uncertainty around monetary policy, politics and 
earnings. The next two weeks will be busy for major central 
banks, US political watchers and technology leaders powering 
the AI supercycle. We expect the Fed, ECB and BoJ to hold 
rates next week, but markets will focus on whether central 
banks are turning more hawkish due to the latest surge in fuel 
prices. The US mid -term elections are expected to give 
Democrats control of the House and Senate, with implications 
for taxes and the AI-led investment boom.  
Meanwhile, the consensus is expecting the US tech sector to 
report over 60% y/y earnings growth for Q3, the biggest jump 
in over two decades outside the post-COVID rebound.  Each of 
these three drivers have the potential to surprise markets.  
Big Three to hold monetary policy: We expect the Fed, ECB 
and BoJ to hold policy rates next week, after September’s 
hikes, as they assess whether higher bond yields have 
tightened financial conditions enough to offset fuel-price-driven 
inflation. We expect the Big Three to hike next in December as 
fuel prices are likely to stay elevated into early next year due to 
refining capacity constraints. Nevertheless, the Fed and ECB 
are likely to deliver less than around 60bps of rate hikes priced 
in by money markets over the next 12 months on the back of 
an eventual easing of oil and fuel prices leading to disinflation 
by H2 2027. This outlook means 3 -7-year Developed Market 
investment grade bonds currently offer attractive risk-reward.  
Opportunity in Australian bonds. Other major central banks, 
such as the Bank of England, Bank of Canada and Reserve 
Bank of New Zealand are likely to hike rates by 25bps in the 
next two weeks given their focus on taming near-term inflation. 
However, we believe the Reserve Bank of Australia (RBA) is 
close to the end of its hiking cycle, even if it hikes one last time 
in the coming months (money markets give a 60% chance of a 
hike on 3 November). Australia’s domestic labour and housing 
markets and business confidence are softening after the RBA 
hiked its policy rate four times this year to a 15 -year high. 
Meanwhile, Australia’s cash rate is 60bps higher than the US 
policy rate, supporting the AUD. We continue to see attractive 
income and FX gain opportunities from AUD corporate bonds.  
A ‘Blue Wave’ in the US?  Prediction markets strongly favour 
Democrats taking the House of Representatives and possibly 
the Senate in the 3 November US mid-term elections. While an 
opposition sweep in the mid-terms has historically delivered the 
best 12-month returns for equities  among all sc enarios, partly 
due to the resulting policy gridlock, a decisive Democratic “blue 
wave” could increase longer -term risks of higher taxes and 
uncertainty over policy towards AI investment and data centres. 
With the Senate race still close and prediction markets fa llible, 
we remain cautious on risk assets in the near term.  
Closing EU bank contingent convertible bond s (CoCos1) 
and US 10 -year go vernment bond ideas:  We lock in 2% 
gains in EU CoCos and 1.7% gain in US 10 -year government 
bond ideas amid French and US political risks (page 5-6). We 
continue to hedge any inflation risks through inflation-protected 
bonds and geopolitical risk through an Overweight in gold.  
Watching Big Tech earnings:   Upcoming Big Tech earnings 
raises uncertainty as market leadership remains concentrated 
and share -price reactions more mixed. With valuations less 
likely to drive returns, earnings delivery and sustainability of AI 
investment, funding and monet isation and broadening out of 
earnings beyond tech will be critical, raising the risk of volatility 
and sector divergence.  We remain invested but defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification across sectors and regions.  
Staying defensive and diversified. We recently upgraded the 
defensive US Healthcare sector to Overweight , while retaining 
our Overweights to US tech and materials as well as financials 
in the US, Euro area and Japan. We also downgraded the more 
volatile Communications Services sector to Neutral  and took 
profits on the semiconductor segment earlier this month before 
the latest correction. Our defensive and diversified stance 
should preserve participation in further equity upside, while 
reducing exposure to earnings disappointments and supporting 
resilience through the reporting season.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the key takeaways from the technology sector 
earnings reported so far this week? 
Our view: Recent  technology sector earnings results point to 
strong compute demand and continued capex expansion, 
supporting our positive long -term view on the AI investment 
theme. However, rising funding and execution risks warrant 
near-term caution. We prefer to avoid increasing exposure to 
higher-risk areas within the technology sector for now. 
Rationale: Earnings results reported this week suggest major tech 
companies remain committed to expanding their AI infrastructure. 
While this bodes well for AI infrastructure providers, ambitious 
expansion plans imply that an increasing share of AI capex is likely 
to depend on debt financing. We expect debt-financed AI capex to 
rise from c.25% of AI capex in 2026 to c.40% in 2027.  
Greater reliance on borrowing increases sensitivity to interest rates, 
while deployment delays could postpone revenue recognition amid 
ongoing financing costs. However, evidence of improving AI 
monetisation and adoption should strengthen operating cash 
flows and support internal capex funding. Against this backdrop, 
we will assess Big Tech earnings next week for greater clarity on 
capex guidance and funding and AI monetisation trends.  
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Euro  area bank equities amid 
heightened political and fiscal uncertainty in the region? 
Our view: We remain positive on Euro  area bank equities, 
supported by resilient earnings and shareholder-friendly capital 
return policies. While Fr ance’s fiscal uncertainty and a softer 
growth outlook warrant caution, the recent pullback in Euro  
area bank equities  offers selective opportunities. We favour 
well-capitalised banks with strong deposit franchises and 
diversified earnings. 
Rationale: We expect Euro  area banks’ Q3 2026 net interest 
income to grow 1.5 -3.0% q/q , led by Spanish banks, driven by 
higher rates and continued asset repricing. Resilient fee income from 
wealth management and investment banking activity, as well as 
dividends and buybacks, should provide further support. Price-to-
book (P/B) valuations have eased , despite expectations for 
healthy return on equity (ROE), thus improving risk-reward. 
Fiscal uncertainty in France has widened sovereign spreads, 
pressuring banks’ bond holdings and funding costs . Although 
French banks represent 13% of the Euro  area Bank Index, volatility 
could spread to regional financial markets. However, stress in the 
broader banking system appears limited , supported by strong 
capital buffers and ECB tools to contain disorderly market moves and 
sovereign market contagion. We will continue to monitor credit 
trends, sovereign spreads and banks’ 2027 earnings guidance. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What has driven the recent weakness in  China equities, 
and what is your outlook from here? 
Our view: We maintain China equities as  a Core holding , as 
valuations remain inexpensive. Rapid technological innovation 
and investment in  AI localisation provide tailwinds, but the 
potential for a re-rating appears delayed. Investors should seek 
to diversify their portfolios and reduce concentration risk. 
Rationale: China equities have weak ened, driven by mounting 
scepticism over AI capex returns, structural margin 
compression and concerns about the country’s significant 
financing requirements. Another notable feature in China has been 
significant share issuance, which has diluted earnings per share and 
weighed on the MSCI China Index’s performance. These pressures 
have been compounded by geopolitical headwinds, restrictions on 
advanced chips, an enduring property sect or contraction and weak 
domestic demand. September’s stimulus measures also 
disappointed markets in terms of their scale and pace of delivery. 
Notwithstanding these headwinds, valuations remain inexpensive, 
supported by rapid domestic innovation and structural tailwinds 
in AI localisation , leading us to retain China equities as a Core 
holding. Elevated volatility reinforces the need to be defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification rather than relying on technology 
sector concentration. We also remain Overweight China’s 
healthcare, financial and materials sectors. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Are you still bullish on US 10-year government bonds?   
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish idea on US 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. The risk -reward 
has become less compelling after the yield broke below 5%, the 
lower bound of our three-month forecast range of 5.00-5.25%. 
Rationale: The US 10 -year yield fell  over 17bps to 4.97% on 22 
October – its first move below 5% since 23 September and biggest 
one-day drop since March 2023. A sell-off in AI-related equities and 
renewed Middle East tensions drove investors towards safe havens. 
Earlier this month, the yield hit its highest level in over two decades. 
The forces behind the rally are fragile, though.  Oil prices remain 
elevated amid escalating Middle East tensions, and September US 
consumer inflation rose to 3.7% y/y . Fed officials  have signalled 
there is no urgency to raise rates at the 27-28 October meeting, 
but persistent price pressures keep further tightening on the table. 
We prefer to lock in gains and close our opportunistic bullish idea on 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is your outlook for French bank bonds , especially 
ahead of France’s upcoming sovereign credit rating reviews?   
Our view: The recent sell-off in French bank bonds is a repricing 
of tail risks, not a credit story. We expect further price volatility 
until the April 2027 French presidential election, particularly at 
the junior end of the capital structure, so we are closing our 
opportunistic EU bank contingent convertibles (CoCos1) idea at 
a gain of 2.0%. 
Rationale: We believe the recent sell -off in French bank bonds 
reflects domestic political and fiscal risks rather than weaker 
credit fundamentals . The sustained widening of the OAT -Bund 
spread has raised concerns about the profit and loss (P&L) impact 
on banks' sovereign debt holdings. Banks provide limited 
disclosure on these exposures. However, banks’ investment 
portfolios are generally diversified, and holdings booked at amortised 
cost should soften the P&L impact. Near -term catalysts include the 
upcoming sovereign credit rating rev iews. A sovereign downgrade 
could trigger bank rating downgrades, with domestically focused 
lenders potentially more exposed than regionally diversified peers. A 
sovereign credit event is not our base case, but we expect headline 
risks to persist until the  French presidential election . Against this 
backdrop, we are closing our opportunistic EU bank CoCos idea. 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Will the November US mid-term elections and European 
bond volatility support a sustained Swiss franc (CHF) rebound?  
Our view: We expect a near -term CHF rebound, supported 
mainly by safe -haven demand amid European bond volatility. 
We believe the US mid-term elections will be a secondary driver. 
Rationale: European sovereign bond volatility provides the stronger 
catalyst for CHF strength. The Fr ance-Germany government bond 
yield spread remains elevated near 160bps, reflecting heightened 
French fiscal concerns and reinforcing the CHF’s safe-haven appeal. 
The US mid-terms could provide additional support for the CHF. 
A Democratic victory in either chamber is expected to  constrain 
further fiscal expansion, potentially weighing on the USD. However, 
this would not necessarily lead to faster fiscal consolidation. 
The SNB’s 0% policy rate leaves  the CHF at a sizeable interest -
rate disadvantage vs. the USD, constraining sustained CHF gains. 
With EUR/CHF below 0.90 , excessive CHF strength could also 
increase the risk of SNB intervention . A decisive USD/CHF break 
below 0.8130 would strengthen the technical case for a more 
sustained CHF rebound.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

(IG) bond option-adjusted spread (OAS) suggest a 
repricing of political and fiscal risk rather than 
credit risk

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would worsening budget deficits and political uncertainty 
across parts of Europe affect your view on EUR/USD and Euro 
area government bond yields? 
Our view: Fiscal and political concerns constrain near -term 
EUR/USD gains, with France being the primary vulnerability. We 
expect EUR/USD to recover towards the 1.17-1.18 range over 
the next  12 months, supported by continued Euro area 
economic growth and an expected fading of USD support. We 
expect wider differentials in Euro area government bond yields, 
with political risk premium elevated in Fr ench government 
bonds (OATs) and German government bonds (Bunds) 
supported by safe-haven demand. 
Rationale: France's high  public debt, large budget deficit and 
political obstacles to fiscal adjustment s are  weighing on market 
confidence, with the country’s  10-year government bond yield 
rising above 5%. The risk of stress spreading to Italy and Spain 
adds to regional concerns, although current evidence does not 
establish a broad European fiscal crisis. German government bonds 
should benefit from safe-haven demand, while fiscal concerns keep 
risk premi a on vulnerable countries' bonds elevated. EUR/USD 
remains more sensitive to US-Germany interest-rate differentials 
and broader market confidence than to French bond yields alone. 
France’s fiscal risks, e levated energy costs and high US interest 
rates weigh on the EUR in the near term. Over the next 12 months, 
Germany’s multi -year public investment programme, resilient 
exports and sound banking-sector fundamentals are expected to 
support Euro area growth. We expect the Fed's rate-hiking cycle to 
peak by mid -2027, reducing USD support and driving  the EUR  
towards 1.17-1.18 . 
The principal tail risk  is fiscal stress widening government bond 
yield spreads and spilling over into bank funding  conditions and 
lending activity, weakening growth and delaying the EUR's recovery. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

## Document 372: Sukuk_ Structure, stability and strategic opportunity (for Private Bank only)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sukuk_ Structure, stability and strategic opportunity (for Private Bank only).pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Thematic Reports)
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 15
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

WS Global CIO Office

InvesTips

Sukuk: Structure,  
stability and strategic 
opportunity

September 2026

### Page 2

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026  2

Steve Brice 
Global Chief Investment Officer

Manpreet Gill 
Chief Investment Officer, EMEA

Hannah Chew 
Portfolio Strategist

Islamic finance is growing at a rapid pace, driven 
primarily by increasing demand for investors who 
seek Shariah -compliant investments. Sukuk, often 
referred to as Islamic bonds, surpassed USD 1trn in 
2025, making them one of the fastest -growing 
segments o f Islamic finance. The asset class’s 
unique value proposition is driving its popularity, 
especially in an increasingly volatile global  
economic landscape. 
In this publication, we will explore the evolving role of Sukuk in wealth management, 
examining market fundamentals, risk frameworks and emerging opportunities within 
this market.

### Page 3

InvesTips: Sukuk: Structure, stability and strategic opportunity  |  September 2026  3

Sukuk, commonly referred to as Islamic bonds, are Shariah-compliant investment 
certificates representing ownership interests in underlying assets, usufructs (the 
right to use an asset and receive its benefits, without owning it) or ventures. In 
return, Sukuk holders receive periodic distributions, typically in the form of rental 
payments (Ijarah), profit-sharing (Mudarabah) or asset-generated cash flows, and 
upon maturity the principal amount is repaid. Sukuk differ from conventional 
bonds in several ways: 
Fig. 1  
Sukuk vs. conventional bonds

Aspect  Conventional bonds Sukuk

Core principle  Lending (time-value of money) Asset ownership 
Underlying asset  Any asset, business or project Must be Sharia-compliant 
Returns  Guaranteed (fixed or floating interest) Not legally guaranteed (depends on 
underlying asset’s performance) 
Risk   Issuer bears all performance risk Shared risk between parties 
Transparency  Moderate (depends on disclosure) High (asset identification required)

Source: Standard Chartered

The role of Sukuk in the investing landscape is evolving amid 
global uncertainty and rising geopolitical tensions. Investors 
are increasingly seeking more stable and asset ‑backed 
alternatives. An investor can consider investing in Sukuk for 
the following reasons: 
Resilient 
Sukuk provide asset -backed collateral that conventional 
bonds often lack, offering a cushion during volatile market 
conditions. Historically, Islamic investments, including Sukuk’, 
have been countercyclical, i.e. they tend to expand or remain 
stable when c onventional markets contract. Across major 
market stress from the 2018 US-China Trade War through the 
2026 Middle East Conflict, Sukuk bonds consistently 
outperformed Global Bonds — delivering smaller drawdowns 
in risk-off episodes such as the 2018 US-China trade war and 
2022 higher for longer inflation, and greater resilience in 
geopolitical oil shocks, underscoring the asset class's 
structural defensive characteristics.

1 The evolving role of Sukuk

Strong fundamentals
The Sukuk market experienced an unprecedented 25% growth in issuance in 2025, with issuance nearly USD 300bn. According
to some  reports,  the  Sukuk  market  could  reach  USD  3trn  by  2030  driven  by  increased  demand  for  Shariah-compliant
investments, sovereign funds’ diversification needs, development of regulatory frameworks for Sukuk and growth in Middle East

capital markets.  Nearly  80%  of  Fitch ‑rated Sukuk  carry  investment ‑grade ratings  and  have  little  or  no  history  of  default,
underscoring the sector’s strong fundamentals. These factors should help to instil confidence among investors, who can look at
Sukuk as central building blocks of their portfolio.
Fig. 3
Sukuk market size in USD bn
Source: The Business Research Company, Standard Chartered
